Proof of
Proposition

12.3
.

To illustrate the basic ideas, we will only prove (
12.7
).

First, note that the expectation is independent of the choice of
(\alpha,\beta)
.
Therefore, we may first sum over
\alpha\in{\mathsf{A}}_{i}
with
\alpha\neq\beta
to compute
the corresponding coefficient.

We will use that for
|z\pm 2|\leq N^{-{\mathfrak{g}}}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}m_{\rm sc}(z)&=-1+\OO\!\left(\sqrt{|z-2|}\right)=-1+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{g}}/2}),\\
m_{d}(z)&=-\frac{d-1}{d-2}+\OO\!\left(\sqrt{|z-2|}\right)=-\frac{d-1}{d-2}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{g}}/2}).\end{split}

(12.12)

Moreover, on the event
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})=1
, the vertex
o
has a tree neighborhood of
radius
{\mathfrak{R}}
. In particular,

\mu=d(d-1)^{\ell},\qquad|{\mathsf{A}}_{i}|=(d-1)^{\ell+1},

and
L^{(i)}_{l_{\beta}l_{\beta}}
as well as
L^{(i)}_{l_{\alpha}l_{\beta}}
are given
explicitly by the Green’s function on the
d
-regular tree (see
Proposition

3.3
).
A direct computation then yields the following identity, whose proof we omit:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in{\mathsf{A}}_{i},\ \beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\\
\alpha\neq\beta\end{subarray}}\frac{m_{\rm sc}^{2(\ell+1)}(z)\bigl(L_{l_{\beta}l_{\beta}}^{(i)}+L_{l_{\alpha}l_{\beta}}^{(i)}\bigr)}{(d-1)^{\ell+2}}=\left(\frac{d+2}{d-2}-\frac{d(d-1)^{\ell}}{d-2}-(\ell+1)+\OO\bigl((d-1)^{-\ell}\bigr)\right).

(12.13)

By plugging (
12.13
) into (
12.4
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\eqref{e:second erm0}&=\left(\frac{d+2}{d-2}-\frac{d(d-1)^{\ell}}{d-2}-(\ell+1)+\OO((d-1)^{-\ell})\right)\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\mathbb{E}\left[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)(G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})})^{2}\right]\end{split}

(12.14)

Next we show

\displaystyle\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\mathbb{E}\left[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)(G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})})^{2}\right]=\frac{1}{{\mathcal{A}}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)\frac{\partial_{z}m(z)}{N}\right]+\OO\left(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\mathbb{E}[\Psi]\right),

(12.15)

and (
12.7
) follows from combining (
12.14
) and (
12.15
)

If we temporarily ignore the indicator and the averaging over embeddings, writing
(b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha},b_{\beta},c_{\beta})
as
(b,c,b^{\prime},c^{\prime})
, the
above statement (
12.15
) reduces to computing

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(Nd)^{2}}\sum_{b\sim c\atop b^{\prime}\sim c^{\prime}}(G_{cc^{\prime}}^{(bb^{\prime})})^{2}=\frac{1}{{\mathcal{A}}^{2}}\frac{\partial_{z}m(z)}{N}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\Phi).

(12.16)

We start with the Schur complement formula (
2.4
)

\displaystyle G_{cc^{\prime}}^{(bb^{\prime})}=G_{cc^{\prime}}-(G(G|_{\{bb^{\prime}\}})^{-1}G)_{cc^{\prime}},\quad(G|_{\{bb^{\prime}\}})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{G_{bb}G_{b^{\prime}b^{\prime}}-G_{bb^{\prime}}^{2}}\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}G_{b^{\prime}b^{\prime}}&-G_{bb^{\prime}}\\
-G_{bb^{\prime}}&G_{bb}\end{array}\right].

Conditioned on
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})=1
, by (
3.21
) and (
12.12
), the terms

\displaystyle\begin{split}&G_{bc}-\frac{\sqrt{d-1}}{d-2},\quad G_{b^{\prime}c^{\prime}}-\frac{\sqrt{d-1}}{d-2},\quad G_{bb^{\prime}},\quad G_{bc^{\prime}},\quad G_{cb^{\prime}},\quad G_{cc^{\prime}}\\
&G_{bb}+\frac{d-1}{d-2},\quad G_{cc}+\frac{d-1}{d-2},\quad G_{b^{\prime}b^{\prime}}+\frac{d-1}{d-2},\quad G_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}+\frac{d-1}{d-2},\end{split}

(12.17)

are all bounded by
\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}})
.
The Schur complement formula (
2.5
) imply

\displaystyle\begin{split}G_{cc^{\prime}}^{(bb^{\prime})}&=G_{cc^{\prime}}-\frac{G_{cb}G_{bc^{\prime}}}{G_{bb}}-\frac{G_{cb^{\prime}}G_{b^{\prime}c^{\prime}}}{G_{b^{\prime}b^{\prime}}}+\frac{G_{cb}G_{bb^{\prime}}G_{b^{\prime}c^{\prime}}}{G_{bb}G_{b^{\prime}b^{\prime}}}+\OO\left(|G_{bb^{\prime}}|^{2}+|G_{bc^{\prime}}|^{2}+|G_{cb^{\prime}}|^{2}+|G_{cc^{\prime}}|^{2}\right)\\
&=G_{cc^{\prime}}-\frac{G_{bc^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{d-1}}-\frac{G_{cb^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{d-1}}+\frac{G_{bb^{\prime}}}{d-1}+{\mathcal{E}},\end{split}

(12.18)

where
|{\mathcal{E}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}(|G_{bb^{\prime}}|+|G_{bc^{\prime}}|+|G_{cb^{\prime}}|+|G_{cc^{\prime}}|)
.

By plugging (
12.18
) into the first statement in (
12.16
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{1}{(Nd)^{2}}\sum_{b\sim c\atop b^{\prime}\sim c^{\prime}}(G_{cc^{\prime}}^{(bb^{\prime})})^{2}&=\frac{1}{(Nd)^{2}}\sum_{b\sim c\atop b^{\prime}\sim c^{\prime}}\left(G_{cc^{\prime}}-\frac{G_{bc^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{d-1}}-\frac{G_{cb^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{d-1}}+\frac{G_{bb^{\prime}}}{d-1}\right)^{2}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\Phi)\\
&=\frac{1}{{\mathcal{A}}^{2}}\frac{\partial_{z}m(z)}{N}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\Phi).\end{split}

(12.19)

where in the first statement we used the Ward identity (
7.50
) to bound the error; and the second statement follows from repeated using the following relations, we omit further details.

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{d-1}}\sum_{x\sim u}G_{xv}=(HG)_{uv}=zG_{uv}+\delta_{uv}=2G_{uv}+\delta_{uv}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{g}}}|G_{uv}|),\\
&\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{x,y}G^{2}_{xy}=\frac{\Tr[G^{2}]}{N^{2}}=\frac{\partial_{z}m(z)}{N}.\end{split}

(12.20)

∎

13
Error from local resampling

In this section we prove the estimates for the error terms arising from the local resampling used to bound (
7.29
). A key ingredient is the following
punctured-vertex
Ward bound.

Proposition 13.1
.

We take
z\in{\bf D}
(recall from (
1.8
)), and recall the indicator functions

\displaystyle I(\{o,i\},{\mathcal{G}})=A_{oi}\bm{1}({\mathcal{B}}_{{\mathfrak{R}}}(o,{\mathcal{G}})\text{ is a tree}),

from (
7.3
).
Then the following holds for
i,j\sim o
and
i\neq j
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{o\in[\![{N}]\!]}\mathbb{E}[I(\{i,o\},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(o)}_{ij}|^{2}]\lesssim\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi].

(13.1)

We recall the resampling data
{\bf S}=\{(l_{\alpha},a_{\alpha}),(b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha})\}_{\alpha\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}
around
o
from
Section

5
, and let

{\mathcal{F}}:=\{i,o\},\quad{\mathcal{F}}^{+}:=B_{\ell}(o,{\mathcal{G}})\cup\{(l_{\alpha},a_{\alpha}),(b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha})\}_{\alpha\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}=B_{\ell+1}(o,{\mathcal{G}})\cup\{(b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha})\}_{\alpha\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}=({\bf i}^{+},E^{+}),

(13.2)

which contains all the switching edges, see
Figure

4
. We also recall the following indictor function from (
8.6
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&I({\mathcal{F}},{\mathcal{G}}):=A_{io}\prod_{x\in{\mathcal{B}}_{\ell}(o;{\mathcal{G}})}\bm{1}\!\big({\mathcal{B}}_{{\mathfrak{R}}}(x;{\mathcal{G}})\ \text{is a tree}\big)\\
&I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}}):=\prod_{\{x,y\}\in E^{+}}A_{xy}\;\prod_{c\in\{o,c_{\alpha},\cdots,c_{\mu}\}\atop x\in{\mathcal{B}}_{\ell}(c;{\mathcal{G}})}\bm{1}\!\big({\mathcal{B}}_{{\mathfrak{R}}}(x;{\mathcal{G}})\ \text{is a tree}\big)\;\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\neq c^{\prime}\in\{o,c_{1},\cdots,c_{\mu}\}\end{subarray}}\bm{1}\!\big(\dist_{\mathcal{G}}(c,c^{\prime})\geq 3{\mathfrak{R}}\big).\end{split}

(13.3)

As a consequence of
Proposition

13.1
, the following proposition states that during the local resampling, the errors
{\mathcal{E}}
from
Lemma

7.4
(after averaging) are negligible.

Proposition

13.2
follows from
Proposition

13.1
and Schur complement formula (
2.4
).
The proofs of
Proposition

13.1
and
Proposition

13.2
will be given in
Section

13.2
.

Proposition 13.2
.

We take
z\in{\bf D}
, and denote the resampling data
{\bf S}=\{(l_{\alpha},a_{\alpha}),(b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha})\}_{\alpha\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}
, the following holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|]\lesssim(d-1)^{\ell}\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi].\end{split}

(13.4)

13.1
Schur complement formula revisit

Adopt the notations as in the proof of
Lemma

7.4
, and condition on that
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})=1
, so the switching edges have tree neighborhood and are far away from each other. Then the normalized adjacency matrix
\widetilde{H}^{(\mathbb{T})}
of
{\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}}^{(\mathbb{T})}
is in the block form

\displaystyle\widetilde{H}^{(\mathbb{T})}=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\widetilde{H}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{\mathbb{W}}&\widetilde{B}^{\top}\\
\widetilde{B}&\widetilde{H}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{\mathbb{W}^{\complement}}\end{array}\right].

We also denote the Green’s function of
{\mathcal{G}}^{(\mathbb{T})}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{(\mathbb{T})}
as
G^{(\mathbb{T})}
and
\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}
respectively.

In this section, we investigate the error from replacing
\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}
with
G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}
.
We notice that
G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}
can be obtained from
\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}
through the following steps. First, we remove
\mathbb{W}=\{b_{1},b_{2},\cdots,b_{\mu}\}
, which gives
G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}
; we then add
\mathbb{W}\setminus\{b_{\alpha},b_{\beta}\}
back, which gives
G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}
; finally we add
\mathbb{T}
back, which gives
G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}
. The errors from these replacements are explicit, thanks to the Schur complement formulas (
2.4
):

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=(G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}\widetilde{B}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}|_{\mathbb{W}}{\widetilde{B}}^{\top}G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})})_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}},

(13.5)

\displaystyle G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=-(G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}(G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{W}\backslash\{b_{\alpha},b_{\beta}\}})^{-1}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})})_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}},

(13.6)

\displaystyle G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}-G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{T}})^{-1}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})})_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}.

(13.7)

The following lemma provide leading order terms for the replacement errors associated with the above equations (
13.5
), (
13.6
) and (
13.7
).

Lemma 13.3
.

Fix
z\in\bf D
and recall
{\mathcal{F}},{\mathcal{F}}^{+}
from (
13.2
). We denote
{\mathcal{T}}={\mathcal{B}}_{\ell}(o,{\mathcal{G}})
with vertex set
\mathbb{T}
. We assume that
{\mathcal{G}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}}\in\Omega
and
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})=1
. For any indices
\alpha,\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]
, the following holds:

1.

The difference
\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}
, is given by

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=\frac{m_{d}(z)}{d-1}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\left(\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}\right)\left(\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}G_{c_{\beta}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}\right)+{\mathcal{E}},

(13.8)

where
{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}=\{x\neq c_{\gamma}:x\sim b_{\gamma}\text{ in }{\mathcal{G}}\}\cup\{a_{\gamma}\}
, which enumerates the adjacent vertices of
b_{\gamma}
in
{\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}}
, and

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{E}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}(|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}+|G_{c_{\beta}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2})+N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\Phi.

As a consequence of (
13.8
), we have

\displaystyle|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|\lesssim\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}(|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}+|G_{c_{\beta}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2})+N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\Phi.

(13.9)

2.

The difference
G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}
is given by

\displaystyle G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=-\frac{1}{m_{d}(z)}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma}c_{\beta}}+{\mathcal{E}},

(13.10)

where

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{E}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}.

As a consequence of (
13.10
), we have

\displaystyle|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|\lesssim\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}(|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}).

(13.11)

3.

The difference
G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}
is given by

\displaystyle G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}=-\sum_{\dist(x,o)=\ell\atop x\sim x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{T}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d-1}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{x^{\prime}c_{\beta}}+\frac{1}{m_{\rm sc}(z)}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{xc_{\beta}}\right)+{\mathcal{E}},

(13.12)

where

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{E}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\sum_{x\in\mathbb{T}}(|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}x}|^{2}).

As a consequence of (
13.12
), we have

\displaystyle|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}-G_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|\lesssim\sum_{x\in\mathbb{T}}(|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}x}|^{2}).

(13.13)

4.

For any
x\sim b_{\beta}
in
{\mathcal{G}}
, the following holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}-G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|\lesssim\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}(|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma}c_{\beta}}|^{2})+\sum_{y\in\mathbb{T}}(|G_{c_{\alpha}y}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{xy}|^{2}).\end{split}

(13.14)

Proof of
Lemma

13.3
.

We can decompose the right-hand side of (
13.5
) as given by:

\displaystyle I:=\frac{1}{d-1}\sum_{\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}\atop y\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma^{\prime}}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{b_{\gamma}b_{\gamma^{\prime}}}G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{yc_{\beta}},

where
{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}=\{x\neq c_{\gamma}:x\sim b_{\gamma}\text{ in }{\mathcal{G}}\}\cup\{a_{\gamma}\}
.

Since
{\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}}\in\Omega
and
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})=1
, (
5.7
) gives that for
\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]
,
|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{b_{\gamma}b_{\gamma}}-\delta_{\gamma\gamma^{\prime}}m_{d}(z)|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
. The leading order term of
I
is then for pairs
\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}
, giving

\displaystyle I=\frac{m_{d}(z)}{d-1}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\left(\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}\right)\left(\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}G_{c_{\beta}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}\right)+\OO\left(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}\atop y\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma^{\prime}}}|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}||G_{yc_{\beta}}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|\right).

(13.15)

The claim (
13.8
) follows from (
13.15
).

To prove (
13.10
), we start with (
13.6
). Consider

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}(G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}(G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{W}\backslash\{b_{\alpha},b_{\beta}\}})^{-1}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})})_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}\\
&=\sum_{\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}(G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{W}\backslash\{b_{\alpha},b_{\beta}\}})^{-1}_{b_{\gamma}b_{\gamma^{\prime}}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma^{\prime}}c_{\beta}}.\end{split}

(13.16)

The leading order term in (
13.16
) is given by those with
\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\eqref{e:defcE1beta}=\frac{1}{m_{d}(z)}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma}c_{\beta}}+{\mathcal{E}},\\
&{\mathcal{E}}:=\sum_{\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}((G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{W}\backslash\{b_{\alpha},b_{\beta}\}})^{-1}_{b_{\gamma}b_{\gamma^{\prime}}}-\delta_{\gamma\gamma^{\prime}}/m_{d}(z))G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma^{\prime}}c_{\beta}}.\end{split}

(13.17)

Thanks to (
3.26
),
|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma}b_{\gamma^{\prime}}}-m_{d}(z)\delta_{\gamma\gamma^{\prime}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
. Thus

|(G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{W}\backslash\{b_{\alpha},b_{\beta}\}})^{-1}_{b_{\gamma}b_{\gamma^{\prime}}}-\delta_{\gamma\gamma^{\prime}}/m_{d}(z)|\lesssim N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}
, and the error
{\mathcal{E}}
in (
13.17
) is bounded as

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{E}}|\lesssim N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}\sum_{\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}||G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{b_{\gamma^{\prime}}c_{\beta}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}.

(13.18)

The claim (
13.10
) follows from combining (
13.17
) and (
13.18
).

To prove (
13.12
), we can rewrite the right-hand side of (
13.7
) explicitly as

\displaystyle-(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{T}})^{-1}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})})_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=-\sum_{x,y\in\mathbb{T}}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}x}(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{T}})^{-1}_{xy}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}.

(13.19)

Since
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+};{\mathcal{G}})=1
, by (
3.26
), for
x,y\in\mathbb{T}
,
|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{xy}-(H_{\mathbb{T}}-z-m_{\rm sc}(z)\mathbb{I}^{\partial})^{-1}_{xy}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
, where
\mathbb{I}^{\partial}_{xy}=\bm{1}(\dist_{\mathcal{T}}(o,x)=\ell)\delta_{xy}
. Thus we have

\displaystyle|(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|_{\mathbb{T}})_{xy}^{-1}-(H^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}-z-m_{\rm sc}(z)\mathbb{I}^{\partial})_{xy}|\lesssim N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4},\text{ for }x,y\in\mathbb{T},

and

\displaystyle\eqref{e:schur_removeT2}=-\sum_{x,y\in\mathbb{T}}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}x}(H-z-m_{\rm sc}(z)\mathbb{I}^{\partial})_{xy}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}+\OO\left(N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}\sum_{x,y\in\mathbb{T}}|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}x}||G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}|\right).

(13.20)

For the summation over
x,y\in\mathbb{T}
in (
13.20
), if
x\in\mathbb{T}
but
\dist_{\mathcal{T}}(x,o)<\ell
, then
\mathbb{I}^{\partial}_{xy}=0
, and we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\sum_{y\in\mathbb{T}}(H-z-m_{\rm sc}(z)\mathbb{I}^{\partial})_{xy}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}=\sum_{y\in\mathbb{T}}(H-z)_{xy}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}=0,\end{split}

(13.21)

where for the last equality, we used that by the definition of the Green’s function.
Thus by plugging (
13.21
) into (
13.20
), it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{\dist(x,o)<\ell\atop y\in\mathbb{T}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}(H-z-m_{\rm sc}(z)\mathbb{I}^{\partial})_{xy}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}=0.\end{split}

(13.22)

If
x\in\mathbb{T}
and
\dist_{\mathcal{T}}(x,o)=\ell
, we denote the parent node of
x
as
x^{\prime}
. We then have, by the self-consistent equation of
m_{\rm sc}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\sum_{y\in\mathbb{T}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}(H-z-m_{\rm sc}(z)\mathbb{I}^{\partial})_{xy}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{yc_{\beta}}\\
&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d-1}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{x^{\prime}c_{\beta}}-(z+m_{\rm sc}(z))G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{xc_{\beta}}\\
&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d-1}}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{x^{\prime}c_{\beta}}+\frac{1}{m_{\rm sc}(z)}G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{xc_{\beta}}.\end{split}

(13.23)

The claim (
13.12
) follows from plugging (
13.22
) and (
13.23
) into (
13.20
).

The claims (
13.14
) follow from the same arguments as in (
13.10
) and (
13.12
), so we omit the proof.
∎

13.2
Proof of
Proposition

13.1
and
Proposition

13.2

Proof of
Proposition

13.1
.

We can replace the indicator function
I_{o}
by
I({\mathcal{F}},{\mathcal{G}})
by the same argument for
7.1

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{o\in[\![{N}]\!]}\mathbb{E}[|G^{(o)}_{ij}|^{2}]=\frac{1}{Z_{\mathcal{F}}}\sum_{{\bf i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(o)}_{ij}|^{2}\right]+\OO(N^{-1+{\mathfrak{c}}})\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})|\widetilde{G}^{(o)}_{ij}|^{2}\right]+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\mathbb{E}[\Phi]).\end{split}

(13.24)

We are now left to write the Green’s function of the switched graph in terms of the original graph. Let
{\mathcal{T}}=B_{\ell}(o,{\mathcal{G}})
and
L:=P({\mathcal{T}},z,m_{\rm sc}(z))
. Here
L
is consistent with the local Green’s function (as defined in (
8.13
)) on
{\mathcal{T}}
, and we use the same symbols to represent them. We notice that since
i,j
are distinct neighbors of
o
, so
i,j
are in different connected components of
{\mathcal{T}}^{(o)}
. Thus
L^{(o)}_{ij}=0
, and
\widetilde{G}^{(o)}_{ij}=\widetilde{G}^{(o)}_{ij}-L^{(o)}_{ij}
. We will use the same argument as in the proof of
Lemma

7.4
. In the rest, we condition on that
{\mathcal{G}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}}\in\Omega
and
I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})=1
. Then by the same argument as for (
7.19
), we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}^{(o)}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\left(L^{(o)}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathfrak{p}}\left((\widetilde{B}^{\top}({\widetilde{G}}^{(\mathbb{T})}-m_{\rm sc}(z))\widetilde{B})L^{(o)}\right)^{k}\right)_{ij}+\OO(N^{-2}).

(13.25)

For any
1\leqslant k\leqslant{\mathfrak{p}}
, the
k
-th term in (
13.25
) is an
\OO(1)
-weighted sum of terms of the following form

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{2k}\in\mathbb{T}}L^{(o)}_{ix_{1}}V_{x_{1}x_{2}}L^{(o)}_{x_{2}x_{3}}V_{x_{3}x_{4}}L^{(o)}_{x_{4}x_{5}}\cdots V_{x_{2k-1}x_{2k}}L^{(o)}_{x_{2k}j}.

(13.26)

Here
V=(\widetilde{B}^{\top}(\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}-m_{\rm sc}(z))\widetilde{B})
, and (
5.7
) gives that
|V_{xy}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
for any
x,y\in\mathbb{T}
.

We recall that
i,j
are in different connected components of
{\mathcal{T}}^{(o)}
. For the sequence of indices
x_{0}:=i,x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{2k},x_{2k+1}:=j
, there exists some pair of consecutively listed vertices that are in different connected components of
{\mathcal{T}}^{(o)}
. If for some
0\leqslant m\leqslant k
,
x_{2m},x_{2m+1}
are in different connected components of
{\mathcal{T}}^{(o)}
, then
L^{(o)}_{x_{2m}x_{2m+1}}=0
and (
13.26
) vanishes. Thus we only need to consider the case that for some
1\leqslant m\leqslant k
,
x_{2m-1},x_{2m}
are in different connected components of
{\mathcal{T}}^{(o)}
.
In this case,

\displaystyle V_{x_{2m-1},x_{2m}}=(\widetilde{B}^{\top}(\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}-m_{\rm sc}(z))\widetilde{B})_{x_{2m-1},x_{2m}}=\frac{1}{d-1}\sum_{\alpha,\beta:l_{\alpha}=x_{2m-1},l_{\beta}=x_{2m}}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}.

(13.27)

If
k=1
, then
m=1
and we can compute (
13.26
) using (
13.27
) as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\frac{1}{d-1}\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}L^{(o)}_{il_{\alpha}}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}L^{(o)}_{l_{\beta}j}=\frac{{\mathfrak{c}}}{(d-1)^{\ell}}\sum_{\dist_{\mathcal{T}}(i,l_{\alpha})=\ell-1\atop\dist_{\mathcal{T}}(j,l_{\beta})=\ell-1}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}},\end{split}

(13.28)

where we used (
7.17
), and
|{\mathfrak{c}}|=\OO(1)
.
For
k\geqslant 2
, we can bound (
13.26
) as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\eqref{e:PUP2}&\lesssim N^{-(k-1){\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta}|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{2m-2}\in\mathbb{T}\atop x_{2m+1},x_{2m+2},\cdots,x_{2k}\in\mathbb{T}}|L^{(o)}_{ix_{1}}|\cdots|L^{(o)}_{x_{2m-2}l_{\alpha}}||L^{(o)}_{l_{\beta}x_{2m+1}}|\cdots|L^{(o)}_{x_{2k}j}|\\
&\lesssim N^{-(k-1){\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta}|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|(d-1)^{\ell}(\ell(d-1)^{\ell})^{k-1}\lesssim\frac{1}{(d-1)^{7(k-1){\mathfrak{b}}/8}}\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta}|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|,\end{split}

(13.29)

where the first statement follows from (
13.27
) and
|V_{xy}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
; the second statement follows from (
7.17
); in the third statement we used
N^{{\mathfrak{b}}}\geqslant(d-1)^{20\ell}
.

The estimates (
13.28
) and (
13.29
) together lead to the following estimate for (
13.25
)

\displaystyle\eqref{e:GooY2}=\frac{m_{\rm sc}^{2\ell}(z)}{(d-1)^{\ell}}\sum_{\alpha\in{\mathsf{A}}_{i}\atop\beta\in{\mathsf{A}}_{j}}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}+\OO\left(\frac{1}{N^{7{\mathfrak{b}}/8}}\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}|\widetilde{G}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(\mathbb{T})}|+N^{-2}\right),

(13.30)

and by plugging (
13.30
) back into (
13.24
) we conclude that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{Z_{\mathcal{F}}}\sum_{{\bf i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(o)}_{ij}|^{2}\right]\lesssim J_{1}+J_{2}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\mathbb{E}[\Phi])\\
&J_{1}:=\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\left|\frac{1}{(d-1)^{\ell}}\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}\right|^{2}\right]\\
&|J_{2}|\lesssim\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}\left(\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}|\widetilde{G}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|^{2}+\Phi\right)\right].\end{split}

(13.31)

Next, we estimate
J_{1}
and
J_{2}
as in (
13.31
). We need to express
\widetilde{G}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}^{(\mathbb{T})}
in terms of the Green’s function of the graph
{\mathcal{G}}
. In this process, any term that can be bounded by
\OO(N^{-\oo(1)}\Phi)
is considered negligible, since it contributes to an error
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)}N^{-\oo(1)}\Phi\right]=\OO(N^{-\oo(1)}\mathbb{E}[\Phi])
.

Thanks to
Lemma

13.3
(combining (
13.9
), (
13.11
) and (
13.13
)), we have

\displaystyle{\widetilde{G}}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}+{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta},

(13.32)

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}|{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta}|&\lesssim\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!],x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}(|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}+|G_{c_{\beta}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2})+\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}(|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}b_{\gamma}}|^{2})\\
&+\sum_{x\in\mathbb{T}}(|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\beta}x}|^{2})+N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\Phi,\end{split}

(13.33)

and
N_{\gamma}=\{x\neq c_{\gamma}:x\sim b_{\gamma}\}\cup\{a_{\gamma}\}
.

In the following we show that for
{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega
, and
\alpha\neq\beta,\alpha^{\prime}\neq\beta^{\prime}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})(|{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta}||G^{(b_{\alpha^{\prime}}b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}|+|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}||{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}|+|{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta}|^{2})\\
&\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{\gamma\in\{\alpha,\beta,\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}\}\atop x\sim b_{\gamma},x\neq c_{\gamma}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(b_{\gamma})}_{c_{\gamma}x}|^{2}+\frac{\Phi}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}},\end{split}

(13.34)

and thus

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})(G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}+{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta})(\overline{G}^{(b_{\alpha^{\prime}}b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}+\overline{{\mathcal{E}}}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\\
&=\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}\overline{G}^{(b_{\alpha^{\prime}}b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}+\OO\left(\sum_{\gamma\in\{\alpha,\beta,\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}\}\atop x\sim b_{\gamma},x\neq c_{\gamma}}\frac{N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(b_{\gamma})}_{c_{\gamma}x}|^{2}+\frac{\Phi}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}}\right).\end{split}

(13.35)

To prove (
13.34
), we start by plugging in the bound of
{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta}
from (
13.33
) into the left-hand side of (
13.34
), after which each term contains three Green’s function entries as factors. We can bound one of them by
N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
using (
3.26
), and the remaining two can be bounded by terms in the form
\{|G_{c_{\gamma}x}^{(b_{\gamma})}|^{2}\}
with
{\gamma\in\{\alpha,\beta,\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}\}},x\sim b_{\gamma},x\neq c_{\gamma}
or can be bounded by
N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi
using (
3.27
). In the following we estimate the following term from (
13.34
), and the other terms can be bounded in the same way, so we omit arguments about them.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!],x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}|G^{(b_{\alpha^{\prime}}b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}|\lesssim\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!],x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2},

(13.36)

where we bound
|G^{(b_{\alpha^{\prime}}b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}
by (
3.26
).
If
\gamma\neq\alpha
, thanks to (
3.27
), we have (ignore the indicator and the averaging over embeddings)

\displaystyle\eqref{e:yizhong}\lesssim\frac{1}{Nd}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha\}\atop x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{b_{\alpha}\sim c_{\alpha}}|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi\lesssim N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}\Phi.

(13.37)

Thus we can reduce (
13.36
) to the case
\gamma=\alpha

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\left(|G_{c_{\alpha}a_{\alpha}}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}+\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha}\atop x\neq c_{\alpha}}|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}\right).

(13.38)

The terms involving
|G_{c_{\alpha}a_{\alpha}}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}
can be bounded by the same way as in (
13.37
).
Next, we show that we can replace
G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}
in (
13.38
) by
G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha})}
.

\displaystyle\begin{split}\eqref{e:GtGG}&=\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha}\atop x\neq c_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+{\mathcal{E}},\\
|{\mathcal{E}}|&\lesssim\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha}\atop x\neq c_{\alpha}}|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}-G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|+\OO(N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}\Phi),\end{split}

(13.39)

where for the bound of
{\mathcal{E}}
, we used that
|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|,|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|\lesssim 1
from (
3.26
).

Thanks to (
13.14
), we can bound the difference
|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}-G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha})}|
by

\displaystyle\left|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}-G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}\right|\leqslant\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha\}}(|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha})}_{xb_{\gamma}}|^{2})+\sum_{y\in\mathbb{T}}(|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}y}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{xy}|^{2}).

(13.40)

By plugging (
13.40
) into (
13.39
), by the same argument as in (
13.37
), we can bound
{\mathcal{E}}
in (
13.39
) as

\displaystyle\frac{N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\left(\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha\}}(|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}|^{2}+|G^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha})}_{xb_{\gamma}}|^{2})+\sum_{y\in\mathbb{T}}(|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}y}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{xy}|^{2})\right)\lesssim N^{-3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}\Phi.

(13.41)

The claim (
13.34
) follows from plugging (
13.37
), (
13.39
) and (
13.41
) into (
13.36
).

For the first term on the right-hand side of (
13.35
), we recall that
\alpha\neq\beta
and
\alpha^{\prime}\neq\beta^{\prime}
. Then either
\{\alpha,\beta\}=\{\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}\}
, or some indices, say
\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}
, only appears once (namely,
\alpha\neq\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}
and
\alpha^{\prime}\neq\alpha,\beta
). Then we can sum over
(b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha})
and
(b_{\alpha^{\prime}},c_{\alpha^{\prime}})
separately (ignoring the indicator function)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\frac{1}{(Nd)^{2}}\sum_{c_{\alpha}\sim b_{\alpha}}\sum_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}\sim b_{\alpha^{\prime}}}G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}\overline{G}^{(b_{\alpha^{\prime}}b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{(Nd)^{2}}\sum_{c_{\alpha}\sim b_{\alpha}}\sum_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}\sim b_{\alpha^{\prime}}}N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}(|G^{(b_{\beta})}_{b_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|+\Phi)|N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}(|G^{(b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{b_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}|+\Phi)|\\
&\lesssim\frac{N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}}{(Nd)^{2}}\sum_{c_{\alpha}\sim b_{\alpha}}\sum_{c_{\alpha^{\prime}}\sim b_{\alpha^{\prime}}}(|G^{(b_{\beta})}_{b_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|^{2}+|G^{(b_{\beta^{\prime}})}_{b_{\alpha^{\prime}}c_{\beta^{\prime}}}|^{2}+\Phi)\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}\Phi,\end{split}

(13.42)

where in the first statement we used (
11.27
); the second statement follows from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality; and the last statement follows from the Ward-identity bound (
3.27
).
Otherwise if
\{\alpha,\beta\}=\{\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}\}
, (
7.49
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|^{2}\lesssim N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi.

(13.43)

We recall that
J_{1}
in (
13.31
) is obtained by averaging (
13.35
) over
\alpha\neq\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]
and
\alpha^{\prime}\neq\beta^{\prime}\in[\![{\mu}]\!]
. By substituting (
13.35
), (
13.42
), and (
13.43
), we conclude that:

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{1}&=\sum_{\alpha\neq\beta\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\frac{1}{(d-1)^{2\ell}Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}|^{2}\right]\\
&+\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\left(\frac{(d-1)^{2\ell}}{N^{{\mathfrak{b}}}}\sum_{\alpha\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq a_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+\frac{\Phi}{N^{{\mathfrak{b}}/2}}\right)\right],\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\left(\frac{1}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}}\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq a_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi\right)\right].\end{split}

(13.44)

where in the last statement we used (
13.43
) and the permutation invariance of the vertices, so that the expectation does not depend on
\alpha
. By the same argument we can also bound
J_{2}
in (
13.31
) as,

\displaystyle J_{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\left(\frac{1}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}}\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq a_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+(d-1)^{2\ell}N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi\right)\right].

(13.45)

By plugging (
13.44
) and (
13.45
) into (
13.31
), we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}\eqref{e:IIGU2}&\lesssim\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}},\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\left(\frac{1}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}}\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq a_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi\right)\right]\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq a_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi]\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}(Nd)}\sum_{b_{\alpha}\sim c_{\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[I(\{c_{\alpha},b_{\alpha}\},{\mathcal{G}})\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq a_{\alpha}}|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi]\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{1+3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}}\sum_{o\in[\![{N}]\!]}\mathbb{E}\left[I(\{i,o\},{\mathcal{G}})|G^{(o)}_{ij}|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi],\end{split}

(13.46)

where in the second statement, we dropped the indicator function
\bm{1}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)
; in the third statement sum over
{\bf i}^{+}\setminus\{b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha}\}
; for the last statement, we used the permutation invariance of the vertices, so that
G_{ij}^{(o)}
and
G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha})}
have the same distribution.

Thus (
13.31
) and (
13.46
) together leads to the following bound

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{o\in[\![{N}]\!]}\mathbb{E}[I(\{i,o\},{\mathcal{G}})|G_{ij}^{(o)}|^{2}]\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{1+3{\mathfrak{b}}/4}}\sum_{o\in[\![{N}]\!]}\mathbb{E}[I(\{i,o\},{\mathcal{G}})|G_{ij}^{(o)}|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi],

and the claim (
13.1
) follows from rearranging.

∎

Proof of Proposition
13.2
.

Thanks to
Lemma

13.3
, we have

\displaystyle{\widetilde{G}}^{(\mathbb{T})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}=G^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}_{c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}}+{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta},

where
{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha\beta}
as in (
13.33
).
The statement follows from showing

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}]\lesssim(d-1)^{\ell}\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}].\\
&\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]\setminus\{\alpha,\beta\}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G_{c_{\alpha}b_{\gamma}}^{(\mathbb{T}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|^{2}]\lesssim(d-1)^{\ell}\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}],\\
&\sum_{x\in\mathbb{T}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha}b_{\beta})}|^{2}]\lesssim(d-1)^{\ell}\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}],\end{split}

(13.47)

where
N_{\gamma}=\{x\neq c_{\gamma}:x\sim b_{\gamma}\}\cup\{a_{\gamma}\}
.

In the following we prove the first statement in (
13.47
), the others are similar, so we omit their proofs.
If
x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}
with
\gamma\neq\alpha
, or
x=a_{\alpha}
, we can first sum over
b_{\alpha}\sim c_{\alpha}
, and (
3.27
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}\lesssim N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi.

(13.48)

Otherwise
x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq c_{\alpha}
.
Thanks to (
3.26
), we have
||G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}-|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}|\lesssim N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}}|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}-G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|
.
We recall the upper bound on
|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}-G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|
from (
13.40
) and (
13.41
), then it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}=\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G^{(b_{\alpha})}_{c_{\alpha}x}|^{2}+\OO(N^{-{\mathfrak{b}}/2}\Phi).

(13.49)

By combining (
13.48
) and (
13.49
), we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phantom{{}={}}\sum_{\gamma\in[\![{\mu}]\!]}\sum_{x\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\gamma}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(\mathbb{T}\mathbb{W})}|^{2}]\\
&\lesssim\sum_{x\sim b_{\alpha},x\neq c_{\alpha}}\frac{1}{Z_{{\mathcal{F}}^{+}}}\sum_{{\bf i}^{+}}\mathbb{E}[I({\mathcal{F}}^{+},{\mathcal{G}})\bm{1}({\mathcal{G}}\in\Omega)|G_{c_{\alpha}x}^{(b_{\alpha})}|^{2}]+(d-1)^{\ell}\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi]\lesssim(d-1)^{\ell}\mathbb{E}[N^{\mathfrak{o}}\Phi].\end{split}

(13.50)

where in the last line we used (
13.1
) to bound the first term; The first claim in (
13.47
) follows from combining (
13.48
) and (
13.50
).
∎
